**[00:30:28.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000578)  
Ang PS po, which is the salaries and wages, personal salary PS, is at P218 billion, which is the largest share, and 20% higher than the previous 2026 NEP.

**[00:30:44.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000579)  
Ang ating pong, sorry, yung pula po ang maintenance and other operating expense, sorry po, MOOE po yun.

**[00:30:57.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000581)

**[00:31:01.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000582)  
At ang capital outlay, ito po yung medyo bumaba kumpara po nung nakaraang 2026 NEP.

**[00:31:08.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000583)  
Ito po ay composed ng P14.5 billion for HPEP and P4.2 billion po doon sa Health System Resilience Project na ito po ay loan natin sa World Bank.

**[00:31:18.180] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000584)

**[00:31:24.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000586)  
Yung ating, kung ating pong i-divide na yung buong 353 budget po ng DOH, ito nga po yung limang pillars.

**[00:31:35.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000587)  
Yung para po doon sa unang pillar na Healthy School Workplace and Communities.

**[00:31:40.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000588)  
Ang malaking portion po dito, or 55.4 per billion, ay yung share po ng PhilHealth Premium to cover the indirect contributor.

**[00:31:48.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000589)  
30.7 million po ang aming nakalap na dami.

**[00:31:54.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000590)  
Ito po ng ating registered indirect contributor.

**[00:31:57.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000591)  
P2.5 billion po for health promotion.

**[00:32:00.180] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000592)  
Short pa rin po ng mandated na 1%, but ito po ay tumaas na significantly compared ng mga nakaraang taon na halos dumoble.

**[00:32:08.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000593)  
And then P1.3 billion po dito sa ating health emergency preparedness.

**[00:32:12.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000594)  
Dahil alam po natin, bunga nitong global warming, marami po tayo ngayong mga na-experience.

**[00:32:19.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000595)  
Kagaya nga po nitong haze, ito pong pagbaha, at saka po itong surge po.

**[00:32:24.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000596)  
Dengue at leptospirosis.

**[00:32:26.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000597)

**[00:32:28.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000598)  
So kung titignan naman po natin yung performance noong 2025,

**[00:32:33.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000599)  
ang ating pong fully immunized ay 2 out of 3 children.

**[00:32:38.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000600)  
Ito po ay nasa 64.54%.

**[00:32:42.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000601)  
Hindi rin po ito umabot noong target natin na 95%.

**[00:32:46.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000602)  
So ito po yung pinagtutuunan ko ng pansin, kasi karamihan po dito ay yung commodities po ay hindi dumating ng oras.

**[00:32:54.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000603)  
Para pagpambabakuna na inaayos na rin po natin.

**[00:32:58.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000604)  
8 out of 10 children, age 2 and below, had adequate height for their age.

**[00:33:03.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000605)  
So ito po naman yung sukatan natin ng stunting.

**[00:33:05.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000606)  
So ito po ay nasa 21.1% na ibaba na po natin kumpara noong 22.5% noong 2023.

**[00:33:14.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000607)  
And then 8 out of 10 households had access to basic water supply.

**[00:33:18.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000608)  
Ito po ay 82.74% ng kabuang household.

**[00:33:22.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000609)  
Medyo falling short pa rin po.

**[00:33:24.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000610)  
Yung 25 target na 94%.

**[00:33:27.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000611)  
At 7 out of 10 Filipinos had access to family planning needs.

**[00:33:32.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000612)  
Ito po ay nasa 72.5%.

**[00:33:34.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000613)  
So ito po ay nahigitan yung ating target na 64.5%.

**[00:33:38.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000614)  
Ang ating pong nabigyan ng TB meds ay 367,000.

**[00:33:43.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000615)  
At ito po ay 85% po ng ating accomplishment, which is our target.

**[00:33:47.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000616)  
At accomplishment target na na-achieve naman po natin.

**[00:33:51.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000617)  
At 100,000 po ang Pilipino.

**[00:33:53.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000618)  
At higit 100,000 po, living with HIV, ang nakatanggap po ng treatment.

**[00:33:58.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000619)  
At ito po ay 66% accomplishment versus our target of 70%.

**[00:34:03.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000620)  
15,000 cancer patients po ang naka-access ng ating essential medicines and palliative care.

**[00:34:10.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000621)

**[00:34:17.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000622)

**[00:34:19.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000623)  
Sa ating pong PILAR 2, which is the Functional Primary Care Services,

**[00:34:23.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000624)

**[00:34:30.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000625)  
So tinahina po namin ito ngayon doon sa ating YACA program,

**[00:34:34.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000626)

**[00:34:41.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000627)  
Ang communicable disease, 6.9 billion.

**[00:34:44.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000628)  
At nakasentro po ito sa TB at HIV.

**[00:34:47.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000629)

**[00:34:53.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000630)

**[00:35:01.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000631)  
Yung 2.7 billion po ay for non-communicable diseases, which includes hypertension, diabetes, mental health.

Again, ito po ay isasama din ni Doktora Bev sa pagpapalawak ng gamot.

**[00:35:13.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000633)

**[00:35:18.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000634)  
Upang suportahan po yung 18 regyon, lalong-lalo na po yung 110 UHM.

**[00:35:23.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000635)

**[00:35:32.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000637)

**[00:35:39.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000638)

**[00:35:43.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000639)

Out of the 300 billion,

**[00:35:53.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000641)  
kasama na po yung 40 million na Pilipinos registered with the YACAP nung nakaraang taon po.

**[00:36:00.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000642)

**[00:36:04.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000643)

**[00:36:09.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000644)

**[00:36:16.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000645)  
So nagsishift na po tayo to e-MA-EFIP.

**[00:36:18.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000646)  
Pinupisaan lang po ito nung August.

**[00:36:21.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000647)  
At sa mula po nung August, mag-iisang buwan.

**[00:36:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000648)  
At sa mula pa lang po, 32,000 na po ang dito natin na i-process sa e-MA-EFIP.

**[00:36:28.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000649)  
At kami po ay nagpapalawig at atin pong inumpisahan sa DOH hospitals.

**[00:36:38.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000651)

**[00:36:42.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000652)  
54,000 cancer patients po ang nakareceive ng Cancer Assistance Fund.

**[00:36:46.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000653)  
Nearly 2,000 health infrastructure projects were completed in the last 2 years, 3 years, 23 to 25.

**[00:36:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000654)  
And almost 5,000 medical equipments were procured.

**[00:36:56.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000655)

Yung ating pong labindalwang DOH hospitals na karagdagan.

**[00:37:17.610] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000659)

**[00:37:24.830] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000661)

**[00:37:42.310] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000663)

**[00:37:48.810] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000664)

**[00:37:54.830] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000665)  
Ito po yung coverage ng PhilHealth at ng initial 55 billion na nabanggit ko kanina.

**[00:38:06.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000666)  
At ito po ay para palawigin along 11 diseases po na nakalinya na dun sa aming roadmap.

**[00:38:12.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000667)  
Again, yung MAIFI po nabigyan ng 24.2 billion.

**[00:38:16.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000668)  
Ang Cancer Assistance Fund ay nabigyan ulit po ng 3.8 billion.

**[00:38:20.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000669)  
Medyo mas mataas ng nakaraang taon.

**[00:38:22.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000670)  
At yung HPEP nga po, bagamat nabawasan, meron pa rin pong 14.5 na ikinocover nga po yung ating 500.

**[00:38:31.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000671)  
114 health facilities, 845 equipment projects, and 345 medical transport vehicles.

**[00:38:37.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000672)  
So ito po yung aming iapila na i-considera po ng ating appropriations or finance committee po

**[00:38:44.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000673)  
na kung pwede pong maibalik dahil po ito'y napaka-importante para nga po palakasin natin yung ating services sa local government unit

**[00:38:53.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000674)  
para hindi po dumadagsa at nabubulunan itong ating mga DOH hospitals.

**[00:38:58.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000675)

**[00:39:00.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000676)  
So yung pillar 4.

**[00:39:01.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000677)  
4 po ay sa information technology.

**[00:39:03.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000678)  
Gaya ng nasabi ko, talagang titignan po natin na magkaroon na po ng central digital health system

**[00:39:09.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000679)  
kung saan nga po merong health information exchange.

**[00:39:12.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000680)  
480 billion po for epidemiology and surveillance, and 222 million po for health statistics

**[00:39:19.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000681)  
na tinitrack din po natin para matiyak na yung pong ating nagawa ay nagbubunga.

**[00:39:25.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000682)

**[00:39:31.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000683)  
And next slide po.

**[00:39:33.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000684)  
Sa pillar 5, sa ating strong institutions, workforce, and sector.

**[00:39:38.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000685)

**[00:39:43.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000686)

**[00:39:50.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000687)

**[00:39:55.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000688)  
At yung ating supply chain management na akin din po tinitingnan kung paano po,

**[00:40:01.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000689)  
i-revive yung ating mga digital systems na di umano yung ano na po, outdated na daw.

**[00:40:08.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000690)  
Pero titingnan ko po kung paano po ito i-update.

**[00:40:11.180] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000691)  
Kasama na rin po dito yung pagbiminta na po ng warehouse at saka yung cold chain po

**[00:40:16.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000692)  
para nga po dito sa ating mga bakuna at para matiyak po na hindi po nasasayang

**[00:40:21.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000693)  
dahil mamaintain po natin yung quality nung pag-re-store nitong ating gamot at bakuna.

**[00:40:27.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000694)  
Next slide.

**[00:40:29.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000695)  
So yun po ang budget po

**[00:40:31.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000696)  
At ang budget po ng ating departamento and its attached agency at bilang isang health sector

**[00:40:36.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000697)  
ang ating pinagtutunan ngayon po ay internally una sa pagtutulungan, una sa pag-usad, una sa pagkilala

**[00:40:45.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000698)  
dahil ang aking push ay hindi siya siloed at dapat nag-uusap-usap lahat ng sector within the

**[00:40:52.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000699)  
all the agencies within the health sector.

**[00:40:54.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000700)  
Para po ito maramdaman ng ating taong bayan isang mabilis, patas at mapagkakatiwala ang health sector.

**[00:41:01.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000701)  
Maraming maraming salamat po.

**[00:41:25.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000702-split-2)

**[00:41:26.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000702-split-4)  
Kasi dati nag-aaway pa yung [unclear], magsusuntukan pa eh. At least I'm happy now that we'll have a better chance of implementation kasi magkasama na.

**[00:41:36.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000703)

**[00:41:39.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000704)

**[00:41:41.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000705)

**[00:41:43.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000706)

**[00:41:48.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000707)

**[00:41:55.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000708)

**[00:42:01.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000709)

**[00:42:04.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000710)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman, and good morning to the DOH family.

**[00:42:08.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000711)  
And congratulations kay Secretary Mercado.

**[00:42:11.680] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000712)  
I've been receiving positive feedback sa pagka-appoint ninyo sa mga nakakilala sa inyo.

**[00:42:20.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000713)  
As I mentioned earlier, Mr. Chairman, mag-focus po yung aking mga tanong sa Universal Healthcare Act na ipinasan ninyo.

**[00:42:28.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000714)  
You have championed this law.

**[00:42:31.100] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000715)  
So since 2017, 18th Congress, until we enacted it into law in 2019, at saka nag-rollout tayo 2020.

**[00:42:44.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000716)  
Ang isang napansin ko lang, since na-rollout, lagi pong papalit-palit yung ating spending plans.

**[00:42:52.320] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000717)  
Kaya ang unang tanong ko, where are we at in the implementation of the Universal Healthcare Act?

**[00:42:59.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000718)  
Kasi po, pupunta namin.

**[00:43:01.100] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000719)  
Medium, babalik tayo ng low.

**[00:43:03.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000720)  
Hanggang ngayon, wala pa po tayo doon sa high.

**[00:43:06.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000721)  
Yung six years, diba, ang timeline yan, ang pag-aalam ko po, after six years, which ito na yan, 2020, 2026,

**[00:43:15.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000722)  
dapat nasa high-end na tayo, ang coverage, lahat na.

**[00:43:19.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000723)  
So nasaan po tayo ngayon?

**[00:43:22.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000724)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:43:25.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000725)  
Medyo noong po no-launch, nagkaroon po ng, hindi inaasahang COVID.

**[00:43:31.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000726)  
Kaya po, medyo na-delay yung ating implementation.

**[00:43:35.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000727)  
Medyo na-pushback ng two to even three years.

**[00:43:38.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000728)  
Yung pong universal coverage, kung yun po yung pagtutuunan, na-achieve po yun.

**[00:43:43.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000729)  
Kasi immediate eligibility na po lahat ng Pilipino.

**[00:43:46.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000730)  
So hindi na po yung kung meron ka lang hulog, siya haka mako-cover.

**[00:43:49.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000731)  
Ang kaso po, yung supply side, yung pong nagbibigay ng mga benepisyo, yung pa po yung kailangan nating pagtuunan.

**[00:43:57.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000732)  
Kasi hindi pa rin po equal yung presence ng mga...

**[00:44:01.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000733)  
accredited health facilities ng PhilHealth na magbigay.

**[00:44:05.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000734)  
Lalo na po yung mga matataas na uri po ng sakit na meron na naman pong coverage.

**[00:44:10.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000735)  
Pero wala pong mga capabilities.

**[00:44:12.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000736)  
Open heart surgery, renal transplant, at mga matataas pa pong mga procedures

**[00:44:17.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000737)  
ng covered ng Z-package ng PhilHealth.

**[00:44:21.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000738)  
Doon po naman sa mga local government units, medyo doon din po nahuulay.

**[00:44:26.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000739)  
Kasi supposedly po, dahil with the devolved nature of our UHC law,

**[00:44:30.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000740)  
ay natuturoan na po sila how to run their local health systems.

**[00:44:37.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000741)  
May maturity grading po tayo.

**[00:44:39.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000742)  
Almost 110 na naman pong probinsya.

**[00:44:42.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000743)  
Ang talagang 110 provinces na nga po.

**[00:44:46.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000744)  
Ang medyo nag-re-reach na po ng maturity.

**[00:44:50.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000745)  
So ngayon po, nagsishift na kami na ang payment po ay pumunta na doon sa isang special healthcare fund

**[00:44:55.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000746)  
para mapagplanuhan po na mas mabuti ng ating local chief executive.

**[00:45:00.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000747)  
sa kanila pong mga health budgets. Pero kailangan pa po ng tulong kasi nga po una na po dyan di pa rin pare-pareho yung kakayanan ng ating mga local health units na papondohan yung kanilang healthcare.

**[00:45:13.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000748)  
Kaya po yung PhilHealth na nagpalawig ng kanilang coverage noong nakaraan to, malaki pong tulong. Kaya marami naman pong mga local health office o mga LCEs ang lumalapit na ngayon medyo kaya-kaya nila dahil kumikita na yung kanilang mga hospital at clinic.

**[00:45:30.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000749)  
So matter lang po of turuan how to budget and how to allocate properly. At yun nga po yung tinitingnan naming tulungan yung mga LGUs.

**[00:45:39.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000750)  
So in short po, hindi pa po natin na-achieve.

**[00:45:43.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000751)  
Hindi ko lang po mabibigyan yung actual percentage base po dun sa ating targets kasi nga po medyo yung mga nangyaring ventrics nag-iba-iba through the years.

**[00:45:54.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000752)  
But yun nga po yung aking plano, sabi ko kay Dr. Bev, na this year by 2028 dapat yung mga local health units or yung local chief executive may mga sarili-sarili ng kanilang SHF at magkaroon na po kami ng proper financing analysis kung magkano po yung talagang kailangan.

**[00:46:13.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000753)  
Para talagang ma-achieve po yung universal healthcare.

**[00:46:15.840] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000754)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[00:46:18.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000755)  
If I may, Mr. Chairman, i-plus ko lang sa screen yung ito yung papalit-palit na yung three spending plans noong 2022.

**[00:46:27.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000756)  
Ayan po, maliwanag na hindi po negligible yung mga figures. Medyo malalaki ito, mga substantial.

**[00:46:36.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000757)  
So ano yung practical purpose ng financing strategies kung palit tayo ng palit ng target?

**[00:46:43.440] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000758)  
Or nami-miss natin yung target. So probably we may need some more serious study.

**[00:46:52.600] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000759)  
Kasi kung lagi natin nami-miss yung target, para saan pa itong ating ginagawang financing strategies? Thank you.

**[00:47:08.200] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000760)  
Chair, sir, as of now, we are tracking our total health expenditure and siguro yung good na nangyari po is,

**[00:47:19.200] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000761)  
if you look at from 2022 to until now, 2025, yung share po ng PhilHealth, tumaas na po to 19% of total health expenditure.

**[00:47:31.880] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000762)  
And the whole government share is up to 46.5%.

**[00:47:37.220] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000763)  
So we have actually managed to reduce out-of-pocket payment share to at 41% by end of 2025.

**[00:47:44.580] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000764)  
So to a certain extent, parang may malaki na yung progress.

**[00:47:49.200] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000765)  
Kumpara sa 10 years ago, that that's around 55%.

**[00:47:52.500] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000766)  
But Sen, we agree with you po that we have to recalibrate this kasi every three years nagbabago.

**[00:48:00.220] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000767)  
But we are now following the PDP plan, which has a target of reducing our OOP to 37% by 2028.

**[00:48:16.360] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000768)  
So 2025 at 41%.

**[00:48:18.700] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000769)  
2022.

**[00:48:19.200] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000770)  
2028 at 37%.

**[00:48:21.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000771)  
And kung ipa-follow po natin yung initial projections dito po sa PDP,

**[00:48:27.700] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000772)  
that amount should be at around 639 billion.

**[00:48:34.180] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000773)  
So medyo aligned naman po sa number na ito, mas mataas lang po ng konti.

**[00:48:39.740] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000774)  
So sir, yun po yung target namin po ni SEC na ma-achieve po.

**[00:48:44.040] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000775)  
Thank you.

**[00:48:44.320] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000776)  
Thank you.

**[00:48:44.840] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000777)  
Pakibalik lang yung table doon sa CHE.

**[00:48:48.340] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000778)  
Current health expenditure kanina.

**[00:48:52.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000779)  
Yung, of course, maganda accomplishment, kasi malaki yung nabawas doon sa out-of-pocket yung household out-of-pocket payment.

**[00:49:00.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000780)  
Nasa 41.2%.

**[00:49:02.320] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000781)  
Dati po ba, ilang percent ito?

**[00:49:06.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000782)  
Sir, 10 years ago, this was at 55%.

**[00:49:09.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000783)  
55.

**[00:49:10.540] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000784)  
Pero ako gusto ko lang ma-educate.

**[00:49:12.560] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000785)  
Kasi pag sinabi natin government schemes and compulsory contributory health care,

**[00:49:17.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000786)  
financing schemes at 46%,

**[00:49:21.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000787)  
ano po ba ito?

**[00:49:24.240] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000788)  
What would constitute itong schemes na ito?

**[00:49:29.740] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000789)  
Chair, it's national government spend, local government spend, and PhilHealth po.

**[00:49:35.240] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000790)  
Kasama na rito yung mga earmark na naka-incorporate sa GAA.

**[00:49:39.920] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000791)  
Kasi doon sa latest Supreme Court ruling,

**[00:49:44.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000792)  
while naka-earmark ito sa Department of Health,

**[00:49:47.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000793)  
hindi naman ito actually, pero nasa GAA yan, nakapasok.

**[00:49:52.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000794)  
So kasama rin ito dito sa 805 billion.

**[00:49:55.640] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000795)  
Lahat-lahat yan.

**[00:49:57.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000796)  
Including the National Expenditure Program na naging GAA.

**[00:50:03.020] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000797)  
So sa tingin ko po, hindi masyadong, sa akin lang,

**[00:50:06.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000798)  
yung misleading may be a wrong word,

**[00:50:09.460] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000799)  
but for the lack of a better word, I will just use it na misleading.

**[00:50:14.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000800)  
Parang, okay.

**[00:50:17.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000801)  
46.5%, pero ito yung contributory health care financing schemes ng gobyerno.

**[00:50:24.800] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000802)  
Pero masyadong bloated, ikanga, kasi kasama na dyan yung PS.

**[00:50:31.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000803)  
Lahat-lahat eh, di ba?

**[00:50:32.040] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000804)  
Kasi pag sinabi nating General Appropriations Act na ito yung items under the DOH budget,

**[00:50:39.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000805)  
pagka nilimit lang natin sa health care program,

**[00:50:44.080] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000806)  
lilit yung 46.5.

**[00:50:46.440] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000807)  
Hindi po ba?

**[00:50:47.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000808)  
So ang target natin actually is to reduce further the household out-of-pocket.

**[00:50:53.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000809)  
Ito yung masakit eh.

**[00:50:54.680] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000810)  
Ito yung target ng UHC.

**[00:50:57.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000811)  
Yung out-of-pocket talaga, halos, huwag naman masiro.

**[00:51:00.120] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000812)  
Palagay ko, hindi natin kaya in our lifetime.

**[00:51:02.460] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000813)  
Pero medyo mataas pa yung 41.2%.

**[00:51:06.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000814)  
Yung voluntary health care payment schemes, partly government din ito eh.

**[00:51:10.360] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000815)  
Like in the Senate, meron kaming Kaiser, government funds din eh.

**[00:51:14.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000816)  
Yung pambayad ng premium.

**[00:51:16.580] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000817)  
Ang siguro,

**[00:51:17.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000818)  
papupuwera lang dito yung mga corporations, private corporations,

**[00:51:21.440] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000819)  
na meron silang sariling health benefits sa kanilang mga employees, sa kanilang mga staff.

**[00:51:27.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000820)  
So, yun.

**[00:51:30.840] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000821)  
I'd like to think na hindi, in practical terms,

**[00:51:36.400] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000822)  
hindi ito talagang ganung ka-accurate, yung 46.5.

**[00:51:40.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000823)  
Now,

**[00:51:44.280] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000824)  
anyway, yung mga earmarks, ano,

**[00:51:46.740] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000825)  
marami tayong mga,

**[00:51:47.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000826)  
mga specials na naipasa.

**[00:51:49.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000827)  
Gusto ko lang pong makakita ng medyo accurate, no?

**[00:51:53.140] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000828)  
Maski mga ballpark lang, hindi naman talaga detalyado.

**[00:51:56.320] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000829)  
Accounting ng mga funding source para sa UHC.

**[00:52:01.180] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000830)  
Like, for example, ano,

**[00:52:02.960] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000831)  
yung total incremental sin tax collection,

**[00:52:06.680] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000832)  
RA 10351, 2012 pa ito.

**[00:52:10.860] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000833)  
Tapos inamento ng tobacco tax law 2019, and so forth.

**[00:52:15.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000834)  
50% nung national government,

**[00:52:17.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000835)  
government share from the income ng PAGCOR,

**[00:52:20.980] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000836)  
dyan din, naka-earmark din sa UHC.

**[00:52:23.460] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000837)  
So, meron po ba tayo?

**[00:52:24.700] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000838)  
Para lang magkaroon kami ng idea, kasi we're talking of the budget,

**[00:52:28.260] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000839)  
and these are, you know,

**[00:52:30.780] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000840)  
ang source nito talaga hindi naman revenues ng government,

**[00:52:34.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000841)  
kundi earmarks ito,

**[00:52:37.060] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000842)  
plus galing sa GOCC like PAGCOR, PCSO,

**[00:52:41.520] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000843)  
and so forth and so on.

**[00:52:43.380] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000844)  
So, meron po ba tayo yung ano nito?

**[00:52:45.680] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000845)  
Konting detalya lang.

**[00:52:53.910] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000846)  
Share for PAGCOR and PCSO,

**[00:52:58.550] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000847)  
based on our records,

**[00:53:01.730] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000848)  
the total that we should be able to claim from 2019

**[00:53:07.250] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000849)  
up to Q2 of 2026 should be

**[00:53:10.610] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000850)  
P127.55 billion po.

**[00:53:15.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000851)  
2019 to?

**[00:53:17.350] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000852)  
2026.

**[00:53:18.490] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000853)  
2026.

**[00:53:19.590] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000854)  
Opo.

**[00:53:19.870] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000855)  
And then there was appropriation in,

**[00:53:23.910] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000856)  
fiscal year 2023 GAA of 21 billion,

**[00:53:28.070] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000857)  
and fiscal year 2024 GAA of 21 billion.

**[00:53:32.830] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000858)  
So, for both of this, we have SARO already, but no.

**[00:53:36.710] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000859)  
Yeah, ang problema is naka-earmark,

**[00:53:39.990] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000860)  
nare-release ba talaga yung total amount na naka-earmark?

**[00:53:42.250] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000861)  
Yun ang problema natin.

**[00:53:44.350] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000862)  
Kasi on paper, magandang pakinggan yung,

**[00:53:47.590] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000863)  
eto yung 50% component

**[00:53:49.470] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000864)  
ng national government share, ano, from PAGCOR.

**[00:53:53.910] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000865)  
Pero yung, in real terms, yung nominal figure

**[00:53:57.710] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000866)  
ng 50% ng share ng government,

**[00:54:01.810] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000867)  
eh, dapat mapunta sa PAGCOR.

**[00:54:04.010] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000868)  
50% ba talaga napunta sa PAGCOR?

**[00:54:06.230] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000869)  
I mean, sorry, sa DOH or sa universal healthcare.

**[00:54:10.770] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000870)  
Ano yung gap?

**[00:54:11.910] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000871)  
From all these special laws na naipasa,

**[00:54:15.870] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000872)  
na naka-earmark dapat sa DOH.

**[00:54:17.570] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000873)  
Of course, you understand na ito,

**[00:54:19.670] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000874)  
sa buong GAA ito pinapasok, hindi ba?

**[00:54:21.670] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000875)  
And you're dependent,

**[00:54:23.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000876)  
you're dependent on the DBM.

**[00:54:26.030] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000877)  
Kung magkano yung maibibigay lang para sa UHC?

**[00:54:29.850] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000878)  
Actually, Senator Lacson, with your indulgence,

**[00:54:31.950] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000879)  
we asked this also during the DBCC.

**[00:54:36.230] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000880)  
Yung po mga earmarked funds,

**[00:54:38.110] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000881)  
please give us also your own record

**[00:54:41.050] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000882)  
so that we can reconcile.

**[00:54:42.870] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000883)  
Kasi may mga, alam ko,

**[00:54:45.030] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000884)  
mataas yung receivable, supposedly,

**[00:54:48.470] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000885)  
ng PhilHealth for the implementation of UHC.

**[00:54:51.510] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000886)  
For some reason,

**[00:54:53.830] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000887)  
parin nila sinasagot bakit hindi nire-remit yung buo

**[00:54:57.590] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000888)  
dahil these are earmarked funds, no?

**[00:55:00.410] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000889)  
And they cannot use this for any purpose.

**[00:55:02.190] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000890)  
Siguro,

**[00:55:03.190] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000891)  
Doc Bev,

**[00:55:06.110] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000892)  
pakian na lang kung ilan po yung sa records po ng PhilHealth

**[00:55:08.450] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000893)  
so that we can reconcile

**[00:55:09.350] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000894)  
and probably pag bumalik yun sa DOF or DBM,

**[00:55:13.470] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000895)  
we can reconcile.

**[00:55:16.950] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000896)  
This is where we can help you, no?

**[00:55:19.250] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000897)  
Kasi ito yung oversight namin eh.

**[00:55:20.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000898)  
Kung hindi nagko-comply yung DBM,

**[00:55:23.830] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000899)  
kasi covered ng batas,

**[00:55:25.710] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000900)  
earmarked para sa DOH, para sa UHC,

**[00:55:28.630] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000901)  
tapos hindi naman binibigay sa inyo.

**[00:55:30.730] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000902)  
This is where our oversight function comes in or kicks in.

**[00:55:34.750] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000903)  
So, yun lang muna, Mr. Chairman,

**[00:55:37.190] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000904)  
para we can give...

**[00:55:39.770] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000905)  
Malamig na ba ulo mo, Senator Raffy?

**[00:55:43.690] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000906)  
Ipapasa ko na sa inyo yung...

**[00:55:45.670] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000907)  
Medyo malamig na.

**[00:55:46.630] Senator Panfilo Lacson** (asr-000908)  
Sige, kayo na po.

**[00:55:48.290] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000909)  
Ano, no,

**[00:55:48.970] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000910)  
Siguro, thank you, Senator Lacson,

**[00:55:50.510] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000911)  
for bringing this up.

**[00:55:52.430] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000912)  
Siguro, we'll wait for that.

**[00:55:53.630] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000913)  
Para at least we will have the...

**[00:55:55.930] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000914)  
Because, ano eh, this would have been used, no?

**[00:55:58.590] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000915)  
For the benefit, para sa mga beneficiaries

**[00:56:02.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000916)  
and the improvement of the packages, no?

**[00:56:06.810] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000917)  
And again, I'd like to reiterate that

**[00:56:09.330] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000918)  
these are earmarked funds

**[00:56:10.530] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000919)  
and these are really intended

**[00:56:11.830] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000920)  
for the use of PhilHealth

**[00:56:14.830] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000921)  
and implementation of UHC.

**[00:56:17.150] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000922)  
So, siguro, we'll just wait for that.

**[00:56:18.970] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000923)  
Senator Tulfo, you may now have the floor.

**[00:56:21.230] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000924)  
Thank you.

**[00:56:22.830] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000925)  
Thank you.

**[00:56:23.630] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000926)  
Thank you, Sen. Ping.

**[00:56:24.210] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000927)  
And thank you, Sen. JV.

**[00:56:27.270] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000928)  
Tulad ng sinabi kanina, time and time again,

**[00:56:29.630] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000929)  
every year, sa budget season tulad nito,

**[00:56:33.110] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000930)  
lagi kong tinatanong yung mga gamot na nag-expire.

**[00:56:37.250] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000931)  
At ang palaging sinasagot po sa atin,

**[00:56:40.470] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000932)  
oversight to yun,

**[00:56:42.390] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000933)  
and then sinusolido yung gamot

**[00:56:44.310] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000934)  
at pinapalitan ng bago.

**[00:56:48.410] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000935)  
Yun po ay pawang kasinungalingan

**[00:56:50.510] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000936)  
dahil meron po akong proof na binabaon.

**[00:56:53.630] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000937)  
In fact, diyan pinabaon palagi sa TALA.

**[00:56:55.530] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000938)  
Pakita nyo nga, ano, ComSec.

**[00:56:58.750] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000939)  
It's a hospital.

**[00:57:00.130] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000940)  
Anong hospital?

**[00:57:03.950] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000941)  
Kanyan, o, pinabaon niyang mga gamot

**[00:57:06.050] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000942)  
by the hundreds of millions.

**[00:57:09.190] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000943)  
Yan, o.

**[00:57:10.410] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000944)  
At meron pa nga kayong disposal committee na binoo.

**[00:57:13.570] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000945)  
So, bakit tayo nagsisinungaling sa taong bayan?

**[00:57:17.250] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000946)  
Napakarami mga kababayan nating mahihirap

**[00:57:20.750] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000947)  
na nakakamatay,

**[00:57:22.110] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000948)  
hindi nakating sa kanilang mga gamot na dapat,

**[00:57:24.330] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000949)  
mga maintenance,

**[00:57:25.870] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000950)  
biotics, et cetera.

**[00:57:28.190] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000951)  
E tapos,

**[00:57:29.590] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000952)  
ito,

**[00:57:30.770] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000953)  
binabaon.

**[00:57:32.570] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000954)  
Why?

**[00:57:33.610] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000955)  
Kasi nga,

**[00:57:34.850] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000956)  
anong nangyari,

**[00:57:35.550] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000957)  
bumibili kayo

**[00:57:36.450] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000958)  
ng pinapaboran

**[00:57:38.410] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000959)  
na supplier

**[00:57:40.030] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000960)  
na mag-expire na.

**[00:57:42.070] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000961)  
Kaya, murang-mura.

**[00:57:43.630] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000962)  
And of course,

**[00:57:44.390] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000963)  
magkakaroon ng kickback.

**[00:57:45.670] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000964)  
Pakisagot po muna,

**[00:57:46.910] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000965)  
DOH,

**[00:57:47.970] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000966)  
bakit po kayo,

**[00:57:49.250] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000967)  
bakit po palagi tayong time and time again,

**[00:57:51.750] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000968)  
every single year,

**[00:57:53.490] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000969)  
walang mintis talaga.

**[00:57:55.010] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000970)  
By the hundreds of millions,

**[00:57:55.870] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000971)  
millions,

**[00:57:56.370] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000972)  
and even billions.

**[00:57:57.490] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000973)  
Bakit po?

**[00:57:58.930] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000974)  
Bakit?

**[00:57:59.470] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000975)  
Wala ho ba tayo talagang tamang sistema na

**[00:58:01.390] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000976)  
for so many years?

**[00:58:02.830] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000977)  
Hindi pa tayo nakaka-buo,

**[00:58:04.610] Senator Raffy T. Tulfo** (asr-000978)

**[01:23:12.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001782)

**[01:23:13.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001788)

**[01:23:14.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001790)

**[01:23:28.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001819)

**[01:23:31.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001825)

**[01:23:32.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001827)  
and I'll flash on the screen

**[01:23:36.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001833)  
So yung ating budget, Secretary, tumataas siya over the years.

**[01:23:51.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001834)  
Total budget is about 7.9 billion pesos.

**[01:23:56.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001835)  
For the first 1,000 days, it's about 4.9 billion pesos.

**[01:24:00.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001836)  
This report is from EDCOM.

**[01:24:02.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001837)  
We have an education commission dito na tinututukan yung health ng bata including the first 1,000 days kasi alam natin formative.

**[01:24:13.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001838)  
Pero despite the increase in budget, nakikita namin na yung coverage is only 65%.

**[01:24:19.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001839)  
Of course, in a perfect world, lahat dapat covered, lalo na yung first 1,000 days.

**[01:24:27.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001840)  
Dapat non-negotiable yung first 1,000 days.

**[01:24:30.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001841)  
I'm sure you know na pag lumampas ng 4 or 5 years old, irreversible na yan.

**[01:24:36.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001842)  
And then the next slide, Table 3, Alena.

**[01:24:43.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001843)  
Merong seven different types of vaccines and medicines na binibigay.

**[01:24:52.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001844)  
At nakita namin na mataasing wastage rate.

**[01:24:58.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001845)  
About 590 million.

**[01:25:00.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001846)  
Ang wastage rate.

**[01:25:02.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001847)  
So different.

**[01:25:03.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001848)  
Iba-iba yung mga wastage rates ng iba't-ibang vaccines.

**[01:25:11.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001849)  
Pero ang total yan, this is only for 2025, is about 500 million.

**[01:25:16.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001850)  
590 million to be exact.

**[01:25:18.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001851)  
So the point I'm trying to make is only 60% of our first 1,000 days na bibigyan ng immunization.

**[01:25:29.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001852)  
And yet,

**[01:25:30.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001853)  
maraming wastage ang nangyayari.

**[01:25:33.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001854)  
About half a billion pesos ang nasasayang.

**[01:25:37.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001855)  
So kung nabigay natin yung half a billion pesos doon sa mga batang yun,

**[01:25:42.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001856)  
obviously, baka ma-70%, 80% ng mga bata ang ma-immunize natin at mabibigyan ng vaccines.

**[01:45:00.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002377)  
So, papasok yan sa YACA program, in other words, from prenatal all the way hanggang mga nakimbata, hanggang mabigyan ng vaccines.

**[01:45:15.140] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002378)

**[01:45:18.920] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002380)

**[01:45:29.380] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002381)

**[01:45:35.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002382)

**[01:45:55.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002383)

**[01:46:04.460] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002385)

**[01:46:06.640] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002386)

**[01:46:11.120] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002387)  
Nung bago pa ho yung Conditional Cash Transfer Program or yung ating Pantawid Pamilang Pilipino Program, actually hanggang ngayon po, kung di ako nagkakamali, kasama dun sa mga sabihin na nating requirements para ma-release yung kanilang cash transfers, may mga indicators on health.

**[01:46:27.580] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002388)

**[01:46:29.380] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002389)

**[01:46:30.660] NNC Executive Director IV and DOH Undersecretary Dr. Albert Francis E. Domingo** (asr-002390)

**[01:52:00.710] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002464)

**[03:16:09.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004577)

**[03:16:26.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004580)

yung 59, 14%-15% doon ang PhilHealth.

**[04:30:55.870] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005054)  
So yung iba galing sa probably tulong pinansyal ng ako indigent ka sa malasakit,

**[04:31:05.990] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005055)  
nag-fall counter doon sa malasakit program, merong DSWD siguro, financial assistance doon sa LGUs, etc.

**[04:31:15.490] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005056)  
So yung question ulit is, ang sasabihin ng tao is ito bang 14-15% lalaki ito by 2027?

**[04:31:25.870] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005057)  
And how do we make sure na lalaki talaga po ito?

**[04:31:31.050] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005058)  
Sige ma'am, thank you.

**[04:31:33.250] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005059)  
But I'm sure before you answer, we wanted to recognize, the chair would recognize our health champion here in the Senate.

**[04:31:42.210] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005060)  
Yes, before you answer, the chair would like to recognize the press of our Senator Pia Pagkaitana.

**[04:31:48.910] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005061)  
Thank you ma'am for joining us. Please proceed.

**[04:31:50.990] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005062)  
Chair, siguro the way to answer your question po is,

**[04:31:56.850] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005063)  
at least for the no-balance billing beds, both in public and private hospitals,

**[04:32:02.190] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005064)  
which are around 60,000 beds, estimate 60,000 beds,

**[04:32:08.270] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005065)  
doon po dapat ang goal namin ay wala po silang out-of-pocket payment.

**[04:32:13.670] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005066)  
Siyempre, kapag napunta na po sila sa semi-private, private rooms,

**[04:32:17.690] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005067)  
then the out-of-pocket payment,

**[04:32:19.890] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005068)  
will hopefully be more predictable.

**[04:32:24.330] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005069)  
So, ibig lang sabihin, hindi natin kaya i-cover yun lahat kasi mga amenities yun.

**[04:32:29.230] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005070)  
Pero ang goal po namin is makapag-negotiate with the hospitals to say,

**[04:32:33.910] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005071)  
baka sa semi-private, pag may ganitong package yung PhilHealth,

**[04:32:37.830] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005072)  
additional 5,000, additional 10,000 siya.

**[04:32:40.810] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005073)  
So, mas predictable instead of yung mabil-shock sila or magulat po sila.

**[04:32:45.070] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005074)  
I'll go to that later, itong zero-balance billing.

**[04:32:49.890] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005075)  
In a previous, I forgot where I heard about PhilHealth's target or desire

**[04:33:00.250] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005076)  
to raise the 14-15% doubling it.

**[04:33:05.290] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005077)  
I don't know if I heard it from you, ma'am, actually,

**[04:33:07.750] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005078)  
but I encountered this article na that's part of the goal,

**[04:33:13.790] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005079)  
yung doblehin from 14-15 maging 25 to actually,

**[04:33:19.890] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005080)  
actually, to 30%.

**[04:33:21.630] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005081)  
And that's part noong pagpapalawak po noong funding natin sa universal healthcare law.

**[04:33:31.810] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005082)  
Is that correct? Is that an accurate statement, ma'am?

**[04:33:35.730] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005083)  
Yes, Chair.

**[04:33:36.690] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005084)  
Yung increasing the PhilHealth share to as much as 30% of the total health expenditure.

**[04:33:43.930] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005085)  
Ngayon po kasi nasa 19% siya.

**[04:33:48.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005086)  
So, okay.

**[04:33:51.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005087)  
That's a welcome development.

**[04:33:54.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005088)  
But puntahan po natin yung sinasabi nyo,

**[04:33:57.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005089)  
60,000 beds sa yung tinatarget ng zero-balance billing.

**[04:34:06.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005090)  
In fact, when we heard about this,

**[04:34:08.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005091)  
a lot of our colleagues here in the Senate were talking,

**[04:34:10.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005092)  
saan ba talaga itong mga zero-balance billing?

**[04:34:13.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005093)  
And every time I go around, may lalapit sa akin,

**[04:34:16.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005094)  
walang makita saan ba itong nakalagay.

**[04:34:20.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005095)  
Tapos, meron naman.

**[04:34:23.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005096)  
But, you know, in fairness, I encountered someone here in Metro Manila,

**[04:34:28.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005097)  
Metro Manila Hospital, na zero-balance po siya.

**[04:34:31.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005098)  
It's just that parang the entire time na I was going around,

**[04:34:36.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005099)  
ang narinig ko po ay dito lang sa Metro Manila.

**[04:34:39.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005100)  
So, itong 60,000 beds, is it scattered around?

**[04:34:44.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005101)  
At ilan po ito?

**[04:34:45.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005102)  
And perhaps the most important part of the question is,

**[04:34:48.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005103)  
ilan na po ba yung nag-benefit?

**[04:34:50.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005104)  
Kasi, kung ilan lamang po, kasi ilan bang pasyente sa Pilipinas?

**[04:34:58.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005105)  
Tapos, kasabihin natin na ilang porsyento ng pasyente yung nakaka-avail lang

**[04:35:04.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005106)  
o makaka-avail lang nito.

**[04:35:06.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005107)  
Kasi parang ang hirap naman din po na mapakinggan ito

**[04:35:12.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005108)  
every time nire-report natin na we are improving.

**[04:35:16.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005109)  
Thank you, ma'am.

**[04:35:17.980] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005110)  
Chair, maybe I'll start po with the breakdown.

**[04:35:20.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005111)  
of the no-balance billing beds.

**[04:35:22.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005112)  
And then, si Sec po can talk about the beneficiaries.

**[04:35:25.300] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005113)  
So, sir, by law po, all of the public hospitals,

**[04:35:29.360] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005114)  
except the GOCC hospitals, 90% of their beds are supposed to be for NBB.

**[04:35:35.080] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005115)  
And then, 10% of private hospital beds are supposed to be NBB.

**[04:35:40.220] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005116)  
So, parang with that breakdown po, at least for the government beds,

**[04:35:46.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005117)  
a total of 59,790

**[04:35:52.000] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005118)  
beds are supposed to be NBB beds in the government.

**[04:35:56.200] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005119)  
And then, for private, that's supposed to be 6,265 beds.

**[04:36:02.900] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005120)  
So, yung total po dapat, meron tayong in-existence na no-balance billing beds

**[04:36:07.400] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005121)  
na 66,055 in the country.

**[04:36:11.280] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005122)  
So, I guess, nag-umpisa po siya na nagtaas yung PhilHealth ng case rates

**[04:36:17.080] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005123)  
to hopefully cover the whole bill.

**[04:36:18.960] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005124)  
And then, supplemented by...

**[04:36:20.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005125)  
Okay. Yung 6,000, yun lang yung sa private?

**[04:36:25.460] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005126)  
Yan po yung mandated nila.

**[04:36:27.740] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005127)  
Because that's mandated.

**[04:36:29.000] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005128)  
Yes, shared accommodation.

**[04:36:34.160] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005129)  
And siguro, sir, we also would like to be very honest din naman na

**[04:36:37.760] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005130)  
to say na in theory, they are mandated to do it.

**[04:36:41.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005131)  
But because for many years, hindi naman din, I guess, commensurate pa yung bayad.

**[04:36:46.300] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005132)  
So, mahirap i-enforce.

**[04:36:47.360] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005133)  
But now, a lot of the private hospitals,

**[04:36:50.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005134)  
are actually already doing it.

**[04:36:52.660] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-005135)  
And we're really serious in partnering with them to make it happen.

**[04:36:56.880] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005136)  
It's good news, no?

**[04:36:58.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005137)  
There are 60,000 beds available.

**[04:37:00.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005138)  
And these are patients na hindi po nangangailangan ng surgery.

**[04:37:08.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005139)  
Kasi parang, yun yung gusto kong maintindihan.

**[04:37:11.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005140)  
Ano yung pinaka-basic, ano lang po niya, no?

**[04:37:14.840] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005141)  
Again, 60,000 is...

**[04:37:18.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005142)  
I mean, compared to our denominator, medyo...

**[04:37:20.640] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005143)  
Malayo yan.

**[04:37:21.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005144)  
And I'm sure we wanted to increase it by...

**[04:37:25.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005145)  
I mean, increase the target by next year.

**[04:37:27.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005146)  
Sir, sige po, sekretary.

**[04:37:30.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005147)  
Mr. Chair, to give context po,

**[04:37:31.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005148)  
ang total bed available po sa Pilipinas ay mga 120,000.

**[04:37:36.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005149)  
So, yun po ay mix na ng DOH, LGU, at saka po ng mga private beds.

**[04:37:42.180] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005150)  
Ang DOH beds po, nakasama yung regional dito sa Manila,

**[04:37:46.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005151)  
at saka yung four GOCC hospitals,

**[04:37:49.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005152)  
are about 3,000.

**[04:37:50.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005153)  
32,400.

**[04:37:52.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005154)  
At ang LGU po ay mga 21,600.

**[04:37:56.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005155)  
So, ang sumatotal po,

**[04:37:59.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005156)  
kalahati ng bed capacity ng buong Pilipinas,

**[04:38:02.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005157)  
ang aming tinatarget na maging NBB.

**[04:38:05.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005158)  
So, ano po yun?

**[04:38:05.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005159)  
Hindi naman po nangangahulugan na lahat po yan,

**[04:38:08.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005160)  
ang strategiya ay magdagdag ng bayad.

**[04:38:12.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005161)  
Kasi inflationary po eh.

**[04:38:13.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005162)  
Kahit kami nagdadagdag, nagtataas din.

**[04:38:16.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005163)  
Yun po ay nature na talaga ng health economy.

**[04:38:20.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005164)  
So, ano po dito, dapat yung strategic purchasing.

**[04:38:23.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005165)  
So, ano po yung strategic purchasing?

**[04:38:25.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005166)  
Dahil kami po yung pinakal-largest purchaser,

**[04:38:28.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005167)  
pwede po kaming makipag-negotiate sa mga hospital.

**[04:38:33.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005168)  
Kaya nga po ngayon, nag-i-engage na po yung hospital,

**[04:38:36.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005169)  
ay dahil napababa na po natin yung turnaround time ng payment.

**[04:38:40.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005170)  
Dati po, ayaw pumayag dahil, eh, anim na buwan bago kayo magbayad eh.

**[04:38:44.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005171)  
Kaya maglalagay kami ng cost of money, di ho ba?

**[04:38:46.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005172)  
Kahit nailagay ko yan sa banko, makumikita na ako ng interest.

**[04:38:50.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005173)  
Kaysa nakabin-bin.

**[04:38:52.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005174)  
So, ngayon po na naka-15 days na kami,

**[04:38:55.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005175)  
nag-i-enter na sila.

**[04:38:56.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005176)  
Sige, pahayag na kami ng NOBI-NBB ngayon.

**[04:38:58.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005177)  
Bukod dun sa tinaas natin yung coverage.

**[04:39:01.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005178)  
Pero isa pa po dun sa aming pinag-iisipan,

**[04:39:03.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005179)  
ay yung upfront payment.

**[04:39:04.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005180)  
Yun nga po yung global budget.

**[04:39:06.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005181)  
Kasi hawak na po nung ating mga hospital, yung pera.

**[04:39:10.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005182)  
So, which means, pwede po nilang gamitin for purchase

**[04:39:13.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005183)  
ng mga equipment, ng mga supplies,

**[04:39:15.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005184)  
para ma-hedge din po nila yung pagtaas ng presyo.

**[04:39:18.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005185)  
At bultuhan,

**[04:39:19.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005186)  
mas maibababa, maninegosyate po nilang

**[04:39:21.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005187)  
ibaba yung presyo ng kanilang mga consumables.

**[04:39:24.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005188)  
So, yun po yung mga statihiya.

**[04:39:26.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005189)  
Kaya po, ang aming tinitingnan,

**[04:39:29.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005190)  
ay yung pong parang kagaya po ng One Hospital Command

**[04:39:32.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005191)  
nung kasagsagan ng COVID.

**[04:39:34.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005192)  
Titingnan po namin,

**[04:39:35.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005193)  
ano po yung sobra na nakapila sa mga government hospital.

**[04:39:40.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005194)  
At kakausapin namin yung private,

**[04:39:41.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005195)  
o yung 10% bed nyo,

**[04:39:43.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005196)  
ipasok natin para ma-offload natin.

**[04:39:46.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005197)  
At bukod dun sa first-liner ng PhilHealth,

**[04:39:48.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005198)  
papasok namin ngayon.

**[04:39:49.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005199)  
Yung MyPip.

**[04:39:50.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005200)  
Kasi po, MyPip, dapat dun sa mga outliers eh.

**[04:39:53.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005201)  
Kasi hindi naman po mako-cover

**[04:39:55.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005202)  
ng lahat ng case rates.

**[04:39:56.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005203)  
Pag Z-Bend, dapat wala nang dagdag yan.

**[04:39:59.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005204)  
Kasi substantial na yung Z-Bend,

**[04:40:00.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005205)  
1 million to 4 million eh.

**[04:40:02.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005206)  
Pero yung mga outliers,

**[04:40:04.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005207)  
dapat po, appendectomy simple,

**[04:40:06.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005208)  
dapat mga 30,000 in ICU.

**[04:40:08.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005209)  
Kasi nagperitonitis,

**[04:40:10.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005210)  
nagkaroon ng komplikasyon.

**[04:40:11.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005211)  
Dun po papasok dapat yung MyPip.

**[04:40:13.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005212)  
So, dapat po rules-based,

**[04:40:15.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005213)  
supplemental ang MyPips

**[04:40:16.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005214)  
dun sa coverage po ng PhilHealth

**[04:40:17.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005215)  
para ma-achieve po natin yung NBB.

**[04:40:19.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005216)  
Negotiated po yung rates

**[04:40:21.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005217)  
dun po sa mga hospital.

**[04:40:23.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005218)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[04:40:25.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005219)  
We appreciate the answer, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:40:29.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005220)  
I'll go to my next topic

**[04:40:31.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005221)  
unless, Mr. Chairman,

**[04:40:32.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005222)  
you wanted to say something

**[04:40:33.780] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005223)  
about the zero billing.

**[04:40:35.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005224)  
As I'm going to the next topic,

**[04:40:37.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005225)  
or Senator Pia would like to...

**[04:40:39.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005226)  
I'm leaving the zero billing

**[04:40:41.400] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005227)  
if you want to...

**[04:40:43.700] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005228)  
I'll go to another topic.

**[04:40:47.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005229)  
I'm sure...

**[04:40:48.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005230)  
I'm sure...

**[04:40:49.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005231)  
Because I was looking at you last time

**[04:40:52.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005232)  
we were together and talking about this.

**[04:40:55.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005233)  
Yes, okay.

**[04:40:55.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005234)  
I'm sure you don't...

**[04:40:56.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005235)  
Very quickly, just to add to the zero billing issue.

**[04:40:59.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005236)  
So, for the 2026 budget,

**[04:41:03.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005237)  
when I chaired the budget of PhilHealth,

**[04:41:07.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005238)  
we included 1 billion.

**[04:41:08.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005239)  
Alam niyo yun?

**[04:41:09.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005240)  
For pilot testing in the LGUs.

**[04:41:12.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005241)  
And I just learned that

**[04:41:14.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-005242)  
that amount is going to be made...

**[05:55:45.570] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007681)  
uh,

**[05:55:47.370] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007682)  
what I know, Your Honour, is that we have already, um,

**[05:55:51.350] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007683)  
uh, issued, uh,

**[05:55:53.850] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007684)

**[05:55:57.990] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007685)

**[05:56:04.100] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007686)  
...

**[05:56:06.880] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-007687)  
...

**[05:56:13.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007688)  
I'll just give you some very basic statistics, Mr.

**[05:56:19.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007689)  
Attorney, uh, Tabakin.

**[05:56:21.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007690)  
Uh, the e-cigarette and vaping prevalence in the country

**[05:56:26.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007691)  
among adolescents, uh, aged 10 to 19 years old,

**[05:56:29.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007692)

**[05:56:35.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007693)  
...

**[05:56:37.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007694)  
Not even 1%.

**[05:56:38.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007695)

**[05:56:44.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007696)  
40%, basically.

**[05:56:46.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007697)

**[05:56:47.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007698)

**[05:56:55.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007699)  
And I want to give you a very quick history, Atty. Tabakin.

**[05:56:58.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007700)  
You can do the research and fill in the gaps.

**[05:57:02.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007701)  
But it's very simple.

**[05:57:03.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007702)  
Vaping is the new product of the tobacco industry.

**[05:57:07.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007703)  
In the 1960s, the tobacco industry used actors in white lab coats

**[05:57:12.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007704)  
to make it appear that certain kinds of cigarettes, including menthol cigarettes, were healthier.

**[05:57:17.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007705)  
And they specifically targeted women.

**[05:57:20.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007706)  
So this is well-documented.

**[05:57:22.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007707)  
And there are a lot of balls there.

**[05:57:23.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007708)  
So now that very same playlist is being used for vapes.

**[05:57:27.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007709)  
In fact, we had to push so hard to ensure that we had policies that prevent doctors

**[05:57:33.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007710)  
from...

**[05:57:33.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007711)  
advocating for vapes.

**[05:57:35.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007712)  
Because the law now that we have, which is a very weakened law,

**[05:57:38.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007713)  
our syntax law, again, sorry to lecture, but this is my baby.

**[05:57:42.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007714)  
And 40% of our youth now is vaping.

**[05:57:49.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007715)  
In the syntax law, which we passed, we said that we lowered the age of access to...

**[05:57:55.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007716)  
Sorry, we increased the age of access to 21 years old.

**[05:57:58.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007717)  
Because the brain is still developing.

**[05:58:00.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007718)  
So we said, not even 18, we make it 21 years old.

**[05:58:02.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007719)  
And then we made it very clear that the jurisdiction is with the FDA.

**[05:58:07.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007720)  
And the third is we banned flavors.

**[05:58:09.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007721)  
Because flavors makes it enticing to not just the youth, but even to adults.

**[05:58:14.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007722)  
So allowed lang doon is menthol and then tobacco flavor.

**[05:58:17.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007723)  
That's it.

**[05:58:18.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007724)  
Shortly after that, barely a year.

**[05:58:22.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007725)  
I'm asking for a year.

**[05:58:24.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007726)  
So the syntax law was passed in January 22, 2020, because we passed the bill,

**[05:58:31.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007727)  
but it became a law in January.

**[05:58:32.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007728)  
And then barely a year and a half later,

**[05:58:37.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007729)  
they passed the vape law that overturned those very important provisions.

**[05:58:42.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007730)  
Where did the country see it?

**[05:58:43.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007731)  
The youth was protected.

**[05:58:45.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007732)  
Their access was made 21 years old.

**[05:58:47.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007733)  
They were put under 18.

**[05:58:48.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007734)  
It's embarrassing.

**[05:58:49.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007735)  
For all the accomplishments, our hardworking diplomats do,

**[05:58:53.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007736)  
WHO, they look up to the Philippines because we do so much good work in paper,

**[05:58:59.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007737)  
in advocacy, and then here we are.

**[05:59:01.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007738)  
We increased the age.

**[05:59:02.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007739)  
We lowered the age to 18.

**[05:59:04.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007740)  
And then the jurisdiction was transferred to DTI.

**[05:59:07.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007741)  
And then the flavors, although it is couched in words that appear to be prohibitive of flavors,

**[05:59:14.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007742)  
but the fact that you open the door, you open it a crack, you open it up,

**[05:59:18.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007743)  
and now all kinds of flavors are available.

**[05:59:20.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007744)  
And we try to work with DTI to prevent the sale of these products online,

**[05:59:25.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007745)  
but they are prevalent online.

**[05:59:27.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007746)  
Anyway, that's the history.

**[05:59:29.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007747)  
What is the challenge we face now?

**[05:59:31.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007748)  
DTI will say,

**[05:59:32.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007749)  
that they have the jurisdiction,

**[05:59:34.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007750)  
but if you look at the mandate of DTI,

**[05:59:37.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007751)  
is there anything in DTI's mandate that says that they have any expertise in health?

**[05:59:44.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007752)  
Any.

**[05:59:45.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007753)  
There is none.

**[05:59:47.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007754)  
So, the exact wording of the law,

**[05:59:50.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007755)  
although to me it's not strong enough because I did not want DTI to have any jurisdiction,

**[05:59:54.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007756)  
it says that it will work with FDA.

**[05:59:58.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007757)  
And I will read the exact wording to you.

**[06:00:02.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007758)  
It says, the DTI, in consultation with FDA, shall set technical standards for safety, consistency, and quality of the products requiring registration.

**[06:00:12.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007759)  
So, this is required of FDA.

**[06:00:13.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007760)  
So, if you're telling me that you're working on this, just goes to show how strong the tobacco and the vape lobby is in this country.

**[06:00:21.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007761)  
Because 2022 pa na pa sa yan, and then we are still working on this.

**[06:00:30.350] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007762)  
So, I want everybody to let that sink in.

**[06:00:32.350] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007763)  
Diba, trabaho tayo ng trabaho, papa-increase natin yung support natin through ZBB or whatever.

**[06:00:41.570] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007764)

**[06:00:49.910] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007765)  
So, if you ask me, because the mandate of FDA and DOH is health, you can take a more proactive step.

**[06:01:00.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007766)

**[06:01:02.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007767)

**[06:01:03.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007768)

**[06:01:07.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007769)  
When this law was being debated, the vape law, which I vehemently oppose because it is a watered-down version of the syntax provisions that we had.

**[06:01:23.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007770)

**[06:01:50.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007777)

**[06:01:52.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007778)

**[06:01:57.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007779)

**[06:02:01.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007780)

**[06:02:06.050] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007782)

**[06:02:09.430] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007783)

**[06:02:15.170] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007785)

So for the benefit of the public, more for the public, but I'm not sure, Secretary,

**[06:03:04.930] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007801)

**[06:03:11.950] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007802)  
years, years ago, two decades ago, that for every, what do you call that, inspector?

Inspector ba tawag doon?

Those who go around checking the-

Inspectors.

**[06:03:34.930] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007809)

**[06:03:38.990] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007810)

**[06:03:42.190] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007811)

**[06:03:46.370] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007812)

**[06:03:49.290] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007813)

**[06:03:56.370] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007814)

**[06:04:09.490] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007817)

**[06:04:37.910] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007825)

**[06:05:33.770] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007834)

**[06:05:34.330] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007835)

**[06:05:34.650] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007836)

**[06:06:37.090] RITM Director Atty. Ana Liza P. Hombrado-Duran** (asr-007859)

Futures Thinking Office.

**[06:09:28.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007914)

**[06:09:50.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007917)

**[06:09:52.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007918)  
Two generations away.

**[06:09:53.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007919)  
Two generations away.

**[06:09:54.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007920)

**[06:10:01.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007921)

**[06:10:06.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007922)

**[06:10:12.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007923)

**[06:10:59.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007940)

**[06:11:52.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007953)

You will...

MyFIP covering the balance with every hospital.

**[06:13:52.390] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007995)

**[06:13:54.870] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007996)

**[06:14:04.690] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008002)

**[06:14:08.410] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008003)

**[06:14:10.430] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008004)
