**[06:14:17.210] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008005)

**[06:14:24.630] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008009)

**[06:14:27.670] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008010)

**[06:14:28.370] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008011)  
And then finally...

**[06:14:29.490] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008012)  
Final point on MyFIP.

**[06:14:36.710] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008014)

**[06:14:42.670] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008015)

**[06:14:44.410] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008016)

**[06:14:46.010] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008017)

**[06:14:48.550] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008018)

**[06:14:49.890] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008019)

**[06:14:53.490] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008020)  
Well, for the information of the public, which I've said in another venue,

**[06:14:57.650] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008021)  
but my colleagues know,

**[06:14:58.550] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008022)  
me, my brother...

**[06:15:00.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008023)  
And I think all the members of the minority are now, our recommendations are no longer accepted by DOH.

**[06:15:07.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008024)

**[06:15:13.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008025)  
I'm letting you know because my question is why?

**[06:15:16.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008026)

**[06:15:24.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008027)

**[06:15:26.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008028)

**[06:15:35.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008029)

**[06:15:39.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008030)

**[06:15:43.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008031)

**[06:15:52.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008032)

**[06:15:56.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008033)

**[06:16:04.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008034)

**[06:16:15.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008035)  
Did I say anything here that is unfair to the administration?

**[06:16:19.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008036)

**[06:16:20.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008037)

**[06:16:24.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008038)  
I don't make any personal attacks.

**[06:16:26.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008039)

**[06:16:30.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-008040)

**[06:18:25.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008063)  
Sir, you have the floor.

**[06:18:26.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008064)  
Let us know how much your reduction in subsidy, and then which services will be affected, and what services you need, how much would you need, at least, so that these services and other programs will not be affected.

**[06:18:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008065)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair, Your Honor, Senator Pia.

**[06:18:46.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008066)  
Good afternoon.

**[06:18:48.240] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008067)  
Actually, the subsidy that was given to NKTI for 2027.

**[06:18:55.560] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008068)  
If you will look.

**[06:18:56.220] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008069)  
At the actual comparison of 2026, 2027, there was an addition, and this was meant for special projects.

**[06:19:03.440] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008070)  
And those special projects are currently ongoing, and these are for our expanded outpatient facility building, additional QFS, for increasing number of patients that we are servicing now, with the onset of the zero balance billing that ever since it started, about 11 months ago.

**[06:19:25.540] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008071)  
11 months ago.

**[06:19:25.560] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008072)  
We have already given 72,015 patients to the tune of 1.585 billion.

**[06:19:35.240] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008073)  
So, that addition in 2026 was not included anymore in 2027.

**[06:19:41.980] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008074)  
And therefore, the actual subsidy that will be given to us is enough to cover the remaining QFS for the subsequent year.

**[06:19:54.920] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008075)  
Okay.

**[06:19:55.560] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008076)  
So, that's the first part.

**[06:19:56.000] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008077)  
And then, in addition, we were asked to propose additional subsidies, and this will be the major accomplishments or major programs that we intend to finish by the last part of 2027 or early 2028.

**[06:20:13.760] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008078)  
And this, for the information of everybody, this was the recently announced legacy building, which will complete the phase four of the 2027.

**[06:20:25.560] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008079)  
Which is 300 hemodialysis beds, which are now in the phase three.

**[06:20:29.800] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008080)  
The phase four request will be the completion, which will be now up to the corporate operating budget of NKTI.

**[06:20:38.480] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008081)  
Then, we will convert some of our doctor's clinics to the additional 50 to 60 beds to augment our existing bed capacity, which on any day is almost 100 to 110%.

**[06:20:54.680] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008082)  
Okay.

**[06:20:55.560] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008083)  
And in order to bridge the gap, we are actually converting some of our private rooms, splitting them into two or three to be able to accommodate the service or ward accommodation so that we can accommodate those requesting for ZBB, MyEPIP, PhilHealth, and QFS.

**[06:21:19.200] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008084)  
So our actual bed capacity, which is 370, if I will make a count after we increase the conversion of our patient rooms, will actually be closer to 400.

**[06:21:32.520] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008085)  
And therefore, those are the requests, an additional 300 for our doctor's clinic and conversion of doctor's clinic, another 250 million.

**[06:21:42.240] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008086)  
But that is not in the submitted national expenditure program.

**[06:21:46.980] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008087)  
So this is...

**[06:21:49.200] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008088)  
Where the position of the NKTI is for the 2027 budget.

**[06:21:54.120] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008089)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[06:21:56.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008090)

**[06:22:01.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008091)

**[06:22:03.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008092)

**[06:22:09.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008093)

**[06:22:18.820] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008094)

Additional patients for the...

**[06:22:20.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008096)  
Additional patients.

**[06:22:21.980] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008097)  
Hemodialysis, which is mostly outpatient.

**[06:22:25.420] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008098)  
But...

**[06:22:25.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008099)

**[06:22:30.300] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008100)

**[06:22:34.780] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008101)  
Even without the leptospirosis, for which we have been accommodating all the complicated ones, the severe ones,

**[06:22:40.880] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008102)  
90% of the leptospirosis patient goes to the NKTI.

**[06:22:44.220] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008103)  
But on any given day, year in, year out,

**[06:22:48.180] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008104)  
majority of the patients in the emergency room are patients who had complications

**[06:22:53.900] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008105)  
undergoing hemodialysis, peritoneal dialysis, and kidney transplant, all across the NCR.

**[06:23:01.100] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008106)  
We will put that in writing, Your Honor.

**[06:23:03.920] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008107)

**[06:23:06.700] NKTI Executive Director Dr. Jose Dante P. Dator** (asr-008108)

On behalf of Lang Center of the Philippines.

**[06:25:24.220] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008148)

**[06:25:30.980] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008149)

**[06:25:33.980] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008150)  
We are able right now to purchase within the quarter our new Linux machine.

**[06:25:41.260] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008151)

**[06:25:47.740] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008152)

**[06:26:06.340] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008153)

**[06:26:14.820] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008154)

**[06:26:17.060] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008155)  
we also have biobanking, our instruments and scopes for biopsy, special procedures.

**[06:26:26.620] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008156)

**[06:26:34.940] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008157)

**[06:26:38.820] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008158)

**[06:26:44.820] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008159)

**[06:26:47.060] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008160)

**[06:26:56.180] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008161)

**[06:27:03.740] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008162)

**[06:27:11.240] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008163)

**[06:27:17.060] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008164)

**[06:27:24.000] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008165)

**[06:27:31.360] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008166)

**[06:27:39.780] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008167)

**[06:27:43.900] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008168)  
Na nag-aapela lang kung puwedeng mapagbigyan po yung aming

**[06:27:47.060] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008169)  
Expansion.

**[06:27:47.940] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008170)  
Because right now, dahil nasunog po yung Lung Center back in 1998,

**[06:27:53.240] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008171)

**[06:27:58.620] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008172)

**[06:28:04.020] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008173)

**[06:28:06.260] Lung Center OIC Executive Director Dr. Jubert P. Benedicto** (asr-008174)
